```java
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

```java
public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```java
public class AllUnique {
    public static boolean allUnique(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null) return true;

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}
```

```java
public class AllUnique {
```
```java
    //@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i && i < arr.length - 1 && 0 <= j && j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ also
    //@ requires arr == null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ pure
    public static boolean allUnique(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null) return true;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= arr.length - 1;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int k, int j; 0 <= k && k < i && i + 1 <= j && j < arr.length; arr[k] != arr[j]);
        //@ loop_invariant 0 <= i && i <= arr.length - 1;
        //@ loop_invariant (\forall int k, int j; 0 <= k && k < i && i + 1 <= j && j < arr.length; arr[k] != arr[j]);
        //@ decreases arr.length - 1 - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j && j <= arr.length;
            //@ maintaining (\forall int k; i <= k && k < j; arr[i] != arr[k]);
            //@ loop_invariant i + 1 <= j && j <= arr.length;
            //@ loop_invariant (\forall int k; i <= k && k < j; arr[i] != arr[k]);
            //@ decreases arr.length - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;

}
```
